    document.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
        const link = event.target.closest('.no-tracking-params');
        if (!link) return;

        event.preventDefault();
        event.stopImmediatePropagation();

        const cleanUrl = link.dataset.cleanUrl;
        window.location.href = cleanUrl;
    }, true);
</script>        <!-- Pricing link end -->

<script>
    var inputModal1 = document.querySelector("#c_phone1");
    var itiModal1 = window.intlTelInput(inputModal1, {
        initialCountry: "in",
        geoIpLookup: function (callback) {
            fetch("https://ipapi.co/json")
                .then(function (res) { return res.json(); })
                .then(function (data) { callback(data.country_code); })
                .catch(function () { callback("us"); });
        },
        utilsScript: "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/intl-tel-input@18.1.1/build/js/utils.js",
        separateDialCode: true,
    });

    inputModal1.addEventListener('input', function () {
        var countryCode = itiModal1.getSelectedCountryData().iso2;
        if (countryCode === 'in') {
            // Check if the first digit is zero and remove it
            if (inputModal1.value.charAt(0) === '0') {
                inputModal1.value = inputModal1.value.substring(1);
            }
        }
    });

</script>

    <!-- magicheight js -->
    <script>
        function setMagicHeight() {
            // Get all card elements
            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.boxes');

            // Find the tallest card
            let maxHeight = 0;
            cards.forEach(card => {
                const cardHeight = card.offsetHeight;
                if (cardHeight > maxHeight) {
                    maxHeight = cardHeight;
                }
            });

            // Set all cards to the tallest height
            cards.forEach(card => {
                card.style.height = `${maxHeight}px`;
            });
        }

        // Call the function to set the magic height
        setMagicHeight();

        // Optionally, you can call this function whenever the window is resized
        window.addEventListener('resize', setMagicHeight);

        // magic js for h2 title
        function setMagicHeight() {
            // Get all card elements
            const title = document.querySelectorAll('.industires-title');

            // Find the tallest card
            let maxHeight = 0;
            title.forEach(card => {
                const titleHeight = card.offsetHeight;
                if (titleHeight > maxHeight) {
                    maxHeight = titleHeight;
                }
            });

            // Set all title to the tallest height
            title.forEach(card => {
                card.style.height = `${maxHeight}px`;
            });
        }

        // Call the function to set the magic height
        setMagicHeight();

        // Optionally, you can call this function whenever the window is resized
        window.addEventListener('resize', setMagicHeight);
    </script>

    <script>
        function setMagicHeight() {
            if (window.innerWidth < 768) return;

            const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.top-content');
            if (!cards.length) return;

            let maxHeight = 0;
            cards.forEach(card => {
                const cardHeight = card.offsetHeight;
                if (cardHeight > maxHeight) {
                    maxHeight = cardHeight;
                }
            });

            cards.forEach(card => {
                card.style.height = `${maxHeight}px`;
            });
        }

        function resetHeights() {
            document.querySelectorAll('.top-content').forEach(card => {
                card.style.height = 'auto';
            });
        }

        // On full page load
        window.addEventListener("load", () => {
            setMagicHeight();
        });

        // On resize
        window.addEventListener("resize", () => {
            resetHeights();
            setMagicHeight();
        });

        // On lazy image load
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
            const imgs = document.querySelectorAll("img[loading='lazy']");
            imgs.forEach(img => {
                img.addEventListener("load", () => {
                    resetHeights();
                    setMagicHeight();
                });
            });
        });
    </script>

    <!-- solution new section -->
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            const sections = document.querySelectorAll(".page-section");
            const navLinks = document.querySelectorAll(".side-nav a");

            // Function to update active class
            function updateActiveLink() {
                let scrollPosition = window.scrollY + 150; // Adjust for better accuracy

                sections.forEach((section, i) => {
                    if (section.offsetTop <= scrollPosition && section.offsetTop + section.offsetHeight > scrollPosition) {
                        document.querySelector(".side-nav a.active")?.classList.remove("active");
                        navLinks[i].classList.add("active");
                    }
                });
            }

            // Scroll event
            window.addEventListener("scroll", updateActiveLink);

            // Smooth scroll on click
            navLinks.forEach(link => {
                link.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
                    e.preventDefault();
                    let targetId = this.getAttribute("href").substring(1);
                    let targetSection = document.getElementById(targetId);

                    window.scrollTo({
                        top: targetSection.offsetTop - 50, // Adjust offset for better positioning
                        behavior: "smooth"
                    });

                    // Update active class instantly on click
                    navLinks.forEach(link => link.classList.remove("active"));
                    this.classList.add("active");
                });
            });

            // Trigger scroll to update active class on page load
            updateActiveLink();
        });
    </script>

    <!-- animation on scroll js for solution section -->
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            const elements = document.querySelectorAll(".animate-on-scroll");

            const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries, observer) => {
                entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (entry.isIntersecting) {
                        entry.target.classList.add("animated", "fadeInUp", "animate__delay-4s");
                        observer.unobserve(entry.target); // Remove observer after animation triggers once
                    }
                });
            }, {
                threshold: 0
            }); // Trigger animation when 20% of the section is visible

            elements.forEach(element => observer.observe(element));
        });
    </script>

    <!-- js to hide Try Vasy Now! cta when section is going to end -->
    <script>
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
            const ctaDesktop = document.querySelector(".side-nav-main .cta");
            const ctaMobile = document.querySelector(".cta.text-center");
            const mainSection = document.querySelector(".on-page-main");

            function checkCTAVisibility() {
                const sectionRect = mainSection.getBoundingClientRect();
                const windowHeight = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight;

                if (sectionRect.bottom < windowHeight) {
                    // End of main section is near - hide CTA
                    ctaDesktop?.classList.add('hide');
                    ctaMobile?.classList.add('hide');
                } else {
                    // Still inside main section - show CTA
                    ctaDesktop?.classList.remove('hide');
                    ctaMobile?.classList.remove('hide');
                }
            }

            window.addEventListener('scroll', checkCTAVisibility);
            window.addEventListener('resize', checkCTAVisibility);
            checkCTAVisibility(); // call once on load
        });
    </script>

        // Tab click: Activate + scroll into view

            tab.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                const bsTab = new bootstrap.Tab(this);
                bsTab.show();
                this.scrollIntoView({
                    behavior: 'smooth',
                    inline: 'center',
                    block: 'nearest'
                });
            });
        });
    </script>

            );

                '[data-vsolution-panel="retail"]'
            );

            const categoryTitle = retailPanel?.querySelector(
                '.vsolutions-category-title'
            );

            const prevButton = retailPanel?.querySelector(
                '.vsolutions-slider-btn-prev'
            );

            const nextButton = retailPanel?.querySelector(
                '.vsolutions-slider-btn-next'
            );

            let retailSwiper = null;


            /* ---------------------------------
               Update Retail category heading
            ---------------------------------- */
            function updateRetailCategoryTitle(swiper, animate = true) {
                if (!categoryTitle || !swiper) {
                    return;
                }

                const activeSlide = swiper.slides[swiper.activeIndex];

                if (!activeSlide) {
                    return;
                }

                const newTitle = activeSlide.dataset.categoryTitle;

                if (!newTitle) {
                    return;
                }

                if (categoryTitle.textContent.trim() === newTitle.trim()) {
                    return;
                }

                if (!animate) {
                    categoryTitle.textContent = newTitle;
                    return;
                }

                categoryTitle.classList.add('is-changing');

                window.setTimeout(function () {
                    categoryTitle.textContent = newTitle;
                    categoryTitle.classList.remove('is-changing');
                }, 150);
            }


            /* ---------------------------------
               Initialize the Retail Swiper
            ---------------------------------- */
            if (retailSwiperElement && typeof Swiper !== 'undefined') {
                retailSwiper = new Swiper(retailSwiperElement, {
                    slidesPerView: 1,
                    slidesPerGroup: 1,
                    spaceBetween: 24,

                    speed: 550,

                    /*
                     * Circular navigation:
                     * last slide → first slide
                     * first slide → last slide
                     */
                    loop: true,
                    loopAdditionalSlides: 1,

                    autoHeight: false,

                    allowTouchMove: true,
                    simulateTouch: true,
                    grabCursor: true,

                    /*
                     * Helps distinguish vertical page scrolling
                     * from horizontal category swiping.
                     */
                    touchAngle: 40,
                    threshold: 7,

                    /*
                     * Do not block the browser's default vertical scrolling.
                     */
                    touchStartPreventDefault: false,
                    touchMoveStopPropagation: false,
                    preventClicks: true,
                    preventClicksPropagation: true,

                    resistance: true,
                    resistanceRatio: 0.65,

                    watchOverflow: false,
                    observer: true,
                    observeParents: true,

                    navigation: {
                        prevEl: prevButton,
                        nextEl: nextButton
                    },

                    keyboard: {
                        enabled: true,
                        onlyInViewport: true
                    },

                    a11y: {
                        enabled: true,
                        prevSlideMessage: 'Previous retail category',
                        nextSlideMessage: 'Next retail category'
                    },

                    on: {
                        init: function () {
                            updateRetailCategoryTitle(this, false);
                        },

                        slideChangeTransitionStart: function () {
                            updateRetailCategoryTitle(this, true);
                        }
                    }
                });
            }


            /* ---------------------------------
               Retail / Manufacturing switcher
            ---------------------------------- */
            function activateSolutionPanel(panelName) {
                switchButtons.forEach(function (button) {
                    const isActive =
                        button.dataset.vsolutionTab === panelName;

                    button.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                    button.setAttribute(
                        'aria-selected',
                        String(isActive)
                    );

                    button.setAttribute(
                        'tabindex',
                        isActive ? '0' : '-1'
                    );
                });

                panels.forEach(function (panel) {
                    const isActive =
                        panel.dataset.vsolutionPanel === panelName;

                    panel.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
                    panel.hidden = !isActive;
                });

                /*
                 * Swiper may need recalculation after returning
                 * from a display:none/hidden panel.
                 */
                if (panelName === 'retail' && retailSwiper) {
                    window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                        retailSwiper.update();
                        retailSwiper.updateSize();
                        retailSwiper.updateSlides();
                        updateRetailCategoryTitle(retailSwiper, false);
                    });
                }
            }

            switchButtons.forEach(function (button) {
                button.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    activateSolutionPanel(
                        this.dataset.vsolutionTab
                    );
                });
            });


            /* ---------------------------------
               Keyboard navigation for tabs
            ---------------------------------- */
            switchButtons.forEach(function (button, index) {
                button.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
                    if (
                        event.key !== 'ArrowLeft' &&
                        event.key !== 'ArrowRight'
                    ) {
                        return;
                    }

                    event.preventDefault();

                    const direction =
                        event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;

                    const nextIndex =
                        (index + direction + switchButtons.length) %
                        switchButtons.length;

                    const nextButton = switchButtons[nextIndex];

                    nextButton.focus();

                    activateSolutionPanel(
                        nextButton.dataset.vsolutionTab
                    );
                });
            });
        });
    </script>
    <script>
        window.VASY_NODES = ["Inventory","POS","Accounting","CRM","Warehouse","Sales","Finance","Analytics"];

        (function () {
            var W = 560,
                H = 460,
                CX = W / 2,
                CY = H / 2,
                R = 158;

            var NODES = window.VASY_NODES || [];
            var reduced = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
            var NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';

            function seeded(s) {
                return function () {
                    s = (s * 1664525 + 1013904223) % 4294967296;
                    return s / 4294967296;
                };
            }

            function initSlideAnimation(slideEl) {
                if (!slideEl || !slideEl.querySelector('.stage')) {
                    return;
                }

                var net = slideEl.querySelector('.net');
                var labelsEl = slideEl.querySelector('.labels');
                var cardOld = slideEl.querySelector('.card-old');
                var cardNew = slideEl.querySelector('.card-new');
                var core = slideEl.querySelector('.core');
                var stage = slideEl.querySelector('.stage');
                var parallax = slideEl.querySelector('.parallax');

                net.innerHTML = '';
                labelsEl.innerHTML = '';

                var points = NODES.map(function (label, i) {
                    var a = (i / NODES.length) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
                    return {
                        label: label,
                        x: CX + Math.cos(a) * R,
                        y: CY + Math.sin(a) * R * 0.82
                    };
                });

                var ringLines = [],
                    spokeLines = [];
                points.forEach(function (p, i) {
                    var next = points[(i + 1) % points.length];
                    ringLines.push(line(p.x, p.y, next.x, next.y, 0.9, i * 40));
                    spokeLines.push(line(p.x, p.y, CX, CY, 0.8, 200 + i * 30));
                });

                function line(x1, y1, x2, y2, w, delay) {
                    var l = document.createElementNS(NS, 'line');
                    l.setAttribute('x1', x1);
                    l.setAttribute('y1', y1);
                    l.setAttribute('x2', x2);
                    l.setAttribute('y2', y2);
                    l.setAttribute('stroke-width', w);
                    l.style.transitionDelay = delay + 'ms';
                    net.appendChild(l);
                    return l;
                }

                var rand = seeded(20260804);
                var particles = [];

                for (var i = 0; i < 96; i++) {
                    var t = points[i % points.length];
                    var p = {
                        ox: CX + 74 + rand() * 70,
                        oy: CY - 6 + rand() * 26 - 13,
                        dx: CX + (rand() - 0.5) * 300,
                        dy: CY + (rand() - 0.5) * 230,
                        nx: t.x + (rand() - 0.5) * 34,
                        ny: t.y + (rand() - 0.5) * 34,
                        size: 1.4 + rand() * 1.9,
                        delay: rand() * 200
                    };

                    var c = document.createElementNS(NS, 'circle');
                    c.setAttribute('r', p.size);
                    c.setAttribute('cx', p.ox);
                    c.setAttribute('cy', p.oy);
                    c.style.transitionDelay = p.delay + 'ms';
                    net.appendChild(c);
                    p.el = c;
                    particles.push(p);
                }

                points.forEach(function (p, i) {
                    var s = document.createElement('span');
                    s.className = 'label';
                    s.textContent = p.label;
                    s.style.left = (p.x / W * 100) + '%';
                    s.style.top = (p.y / H * 100) + '%';
                    s.style.transitionDelay = (i * 30) + 'ms';
                    labelsEl.appendChild(s);
                    p.el = s;
                });

                function setPhase(phase) {
                    cardOld.className = 'glass-card card-old ' + (phase === 0 ? 'show' : phase === 1 ? 'fade' : 'hide');

                    particles.forEach(function (p) {
                        var cx = phase <= 1 ? (phase === 1 ? p.dx : p.ox) : phase === 2 ? p.nx : CX;
                        var cy = phase <= 1 ? (phase === 1 ? p.dy : p.oy) : phase === 2 ? p.ny : CY;
                        p.el.setAttribute('cx', cx);
                        p.el.setAttribute('cy', cy);
                        p.el.style.opacity = (phase >= 1 && phase <= 3) ? (phase === 3 ? 0.5 : 0.85) : 0;
                    });

                    ringLines.forEach(function (l) {
                        l.style.opacity = phase === 2 ? 0.35 : phase === 3 ? 0.12 : 0;
                    });

                    spokeLines.forEach(function (l) {
                        l.style.opacity = phase === 2 ? 0.22 : phase === 3 ? 0.4 : 0;
                    });

                    points.forEach(function (p) {
                        p.el.classList.toggle('on', phase === 2);
                    });

                    core.className = 'core' + (phase === 3 ? ' on' : phase === 4 ? ' burst' : '');
                    cardNew.classList.toggle('on', phase === 4);
                }

                if (slideEl.animationTimers) {
                    slideEl.animationTimers.forEach(clearTimeout);
                }
                slideEl.animationTimers = [];

                if (reduced) {
                    setPhase(4);
                } else {
                    setPhase(0);
                    [
                        [1, 1200],
                        [2, 2200],
                        [3, 4200],
                        [4, 5400]
                    ].forEach(function (t) {
                        var timer = setTimeout(function () {
                            setPhase(t[0]);
                        }, t[1]);
                        slideEl.animationTimers.push(timer);
                    });
                }

                if (!reduced && !stage.dataset.parallaxBound) {
                    stage.dataset.parallaxBound = "true";
                    stage.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
                        var r = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
                        var x = ((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5) * 6;
                        var y = ((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height - 0.5) * 6;
                        parallax.style.transform = 'translate(' + x + 'px,' + y + 'px)';
                    });

                    stage.addEventListener('mouseleave', function () {
                        parallax.style.transform = 'translate(0,0)';
                    });
                }
            }

            function initFYSlideAnimation(slideEl) {
                var scene = slideEl.querySelector('.fy-slide-scene');
                var stage = slideEl.querySelector('.fy-slide-stage');

                if (!scene || !stage) {
                    return;
                }

                var docLayer = stage.querySelector('[data-fy-layer="doc"]');
                var particlesLayer = stage.querySelector('[data-fy-layer="particles"]');
                var networkLayer = stage.querySelector('[data-fy-layer="network"]');
                var coreLayer = stage.querySelector('[data-fy-layer="core"]');
                var dashboardLayer = stage.querySelector('[data-fy-layer="dashboard"]');

                var totalPhases = 5;
                var phaseDuration = 1850;

                if (slideEl.fyAnimationTimer) {
                    clearInterval(slideEl.fyAnimationTimer);
                    slideEl.fyAnimationTimer = null;
                }

                function toggleLayer(element, active) {
                    if (!element) return;
                    element.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
                }

                function renderFYPhase(phase) {
                    toggleLayer(docLayer, phase === 0 || phase === 1);
                    toggleLayer(particlesLayer, phase === 1);
                    toggleLayer(networkLayer, phase === 2 || phase === 3);
                    toggleLayer(coreLayer, phase === 3);
                    toggleLayer(dashboardLayer, phase === 4);

                    if (networkLayer) {
                        networkLayer.classList.toggle('is-converging', phase === 3);
                    }
                }

                if (particlesLayer && particlesLayer.dataset.particlesCreated !== 'true') {
                    particlesLayer.dataset.particlesCreated = 'true';

                    for (var i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
                        var x = 12 + ((i * 37) % 76);
                        var y = 10 + ((i * 53) % 78);
                        var delay = 0.5 + ((i * 13) % 7) / 10;

                        var particle = document.createElement('span');
                        particle.className = 'fy-slide-particle';
                        particle.style.left = x + '%';
                        particle.style.top = y + '%';

                        particle.style.setProperty('--fy-particle-x', (50 - x) + '%');
                        particle.style.setProperty('--fy-particle-y', (50 - y) + '%');
                        particle.style.setProperty('--fy-particle-delay', (delay * 0.35).toFixed(2) + 's');

                        particlesLayer.appendChild(particle);
                    }
                }

                if (!reduced && scene.dataset.parallaxBound !== 'true') {
                    scene.dataset.parallaxBound = 'true';

                    scene.addEventListener('pointermove', function (event) {
                        var bounds = scene.getBoundingClientRect();
                        var pointerX = (((event.clientX - bounds.left) / bounds.width) - 0.5) * 6;
                        var pointerY = (((event.clientY - bounds.top) / bounds.height) - 0.5) * 6;

                        stage.style.setProperty('--fy-px', pointerX.toFixed(2) + 'px');
                        stage.style.setProperty('--fy-py', pointerY.toFixed(2) + 'px');
                        stage.style.setProperty('--fy-rotate-y', (pointerX * 0.6).toFixed(2) + 'deg');
                    });

                    scene.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () {
                        stage.style.setProperty('--fy-px', '0px');
                        stage.style.setProperty('--fy-py', '0px');
                        stage.style.setProperty('--fy-rotate-y', '0deg');
                    });
                }

                if (reduced) {
                    renderFYPhase(4);
                    return;
                }

                var currentPhase = 0;
                renderFYPhase(currentPhase);

                slideEl.fyAnimationTimer = setInterval(function () {
                    currentPhase = (currentPhase + 1) % totalPhases;
                    renderFYPhase(currentPhase);
                }, phaseDuration);
            }

            function stopFYSlideAnimation(slideEl) {
                if (!slideEl) return;
                if (slideEl.fyAnimationTimer) {
                    clearInterval(slideEl.fyAnimationTimer);
                    slideEl.fyAnimationTimer = null;
                }
            }

            function initSystemSlideAnimation(slideEl) {
                if (!slideEl) return;
                var stage = slideEl.querySelector('.system-stage');
                var layer = slideEl.querySelector('.parallax');
                if (!stage || !layer) return;

                if (reduced) return;

                /* ---- mouse parallax ---- */
                if (!stage.dataset.sysParallaxBound) {
                    stage.dataset.sysParallaxBound = "true";
                    var frame = 0;
                    stage.addEventListener('pointermove', function (e) {
                        if (frame) return;
                        frame = requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                            frame = 0;
                            var r = stage.getBoundingClientRect();
                            var nx = (e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2)) / (r.width / 2);
                            var ny = (e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2)) / (r.height / 2);
                            nx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, nx)) * 2.6;
                            ny = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, ny)) * 2.6;
                            layer.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + nx.toFixed(2) + 'px,' + ny.toFixed(2) + 'px,0)';
                        });
                    }, {
                        passive: true
                    });

                    stage.addEventListener('pointerleave', function () {
                        layer.style.transform = 'translate3d(0,0,0)';
                    });
                }

                /* ---- calm rotating KPI values ---- */
                stopSystemSlideAnimation(slideEl);
                slideEl.sysKpiTimers = [];

                var kpis = slideEl.querySelectorAll('.kpi');
                kpis.forEach(function (el) {
                    var valuesStr = el.getAttribute('data-values');
                    if (!valuesStr) return;
                    var values = JSON.parse(valuesStr);
                    var delay = parseInt(el.getAttribute('data-delay'), 10) || 0;
                    var i = 0;
                    var initialTimeout = setTimeout(function () {
                        var interval = setInterval(function () {
                            i = (i + 1) % values.length;
                            el.textContent = values[i];
                            el.classList.remove('swap');
                            void el.offsetWidth; /* restart animation */
                            el.classList.add('swap');
                        }, 4200);
                        slideEl.sysKpiTimers.push(interval);
                    }, delay);
                    slideEl.sysKpiTimers.push(initialTimeout);
                });
            }

            function stopSystemSlideAnimation(slideEl) {
                if (!slideEl) return;
                if (slideEl.sysKpiTimers) {
                    slideEl.sysKpiTimers.forEach(function (t) {
                        clearTimeout(t);
                        clearInterval(t);
                    });
                    slideEl.sysKpiTimers = [];
                }
            }

            function stopVinaSlideAnimation(slideEl) {
                if (!slideEl || !slideEl.vina3RafId) return;
                cancelAnimationFrame(slideEl.vina3RafId);
                slideEl.vina3RafId = null;
            }

            function initVinaSlideAnimation(slideEl) {
                if (!slideEl) return;

                stopVinaSlideAnimation(slideEl);

                var scene = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-scene');
                var shell = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-shell');
                var welcome = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-welcome');
                var conversation = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-conversation');
                var cards = Array.prototype.slice.call(slideEl.querySelectorAll('.vina3-card'));
                var q1 = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-q1');
                var q2 = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-q2');
                var reason1 = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-reason1');
                var reason2 = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-reason2');
                var answer = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-answer');
                var ansA = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-ans-a');
                var ansB = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-ans-b');
                var summary = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-summary');
                var kpis = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-kpis');
                var inputBar = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-input-bar');
                var inputText = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-input-text');
                var send = slideEl.querySelector('.vina3-send');
                var products = Array.prototype.slice.call(slideEl.querySelectorAll('.vina3-product'));

                if (!scene || !shell || !welcome || !conversation) return;

                var reasonSteps1 = ['Retrieving live business data', 'Analysing sales performance', 'Comparing outlet metrics', 'Validating calculations'];
                var reasonSteps2 = ['Searching inventory database', 'Identifying low-stock products', 'Retrieving item codes', 'Checking warehouse quantities'];
                var responseA = 'Koramangala Store is your best performing outlet today.';
                var responseB = 'It contributed 38% of total revenue with the highest average bill value.';
                var typedText = "Give me low-stock products' names with item codes.";
                var LOOP = 20000;
                var T = {
                    greeting: 350,
                    cards: 900,
                    hover: 2600,
                    sent: 3500,
                    reason1: 4000,
                    respond: 6900,
                    kpi: 8200,
                    typeStart: 10400,
                    typeEnd: 12300,
                    send2: 12800,
                    reason2: 13200,
                    products: 16000
                };

                function clamp(v) {
                    return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
                }

                function progress(t, from, to) {
                    return clamp((t - from) / (to - from));
                }

                function ease(v) {
                    return v < .5 ? 4 * v * v * v : 1 - Math.pow(-2 * v + 2, 3) / 2;
                }

                function fade(el, t, from, duration, rise) {
                    if (!el) return 0;
                    var p = ease(progress(t, from, from + duration));
                    el.style.opacity = p;
                    el.style.transform = 'translate3d(0,' + ((1 - p) * (rise || 8)) + 'px,0)';
                    return p;
                }

                function buildReason(host, steps) {
                    var wrap = host.querySelector('.vina3-reason-steps');
                    wrap.innerHTML = '';
                    return steps.map(function (label) {
                        var row = document.createElement('div');
                        row.className = 'vina3-step';
                        row.innerHTML = '<span class="vina3-tick">✓</span><span>' + label + '…</span>';
                        wrap.appendChild(row);
                        return row;
                    });
                }

                var r1 = buildReason(reason1, reasonSteps1);
                var r2 = buildReason(reason2, reasonSteps2);

                kpis.innerHTML = '';
                [
                    ['Today\'s Sales', '₹4,82,300'],
                    ['Average Billing', '₹1,240'],
                    ['Transactions', '389']
                ].forEach(function (item) {
                    var card = document.createElement('div');
                    card.className = 'vina3-kpi';
                    card.innerHTML = '<p class="vina3-kpi-label">' + item[0] + '</p><p class="vina3-kpi-value">' + item[1] + '</p>';
                    kpis.appendChild(card);
                });

                var trend = document.createElement('div');
                trend.className = 'vina3-kpi vina3-kpi-wide';
                trend.innerHTML = '<p class="vina3-kpi-label">Sales Trend</p><svg class="vina3-spark-line" viewBox="0 0 126 40" fill="none"><path d="M0,34 L18,29 L36,31 L54,22 L72,24 L90,14 L108,17 L126,6" stroke="var(--vina3-accent)" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="160" stroke-dashoffset="160"/></svg>';
                kpis.appendChild(trend);

                var growth = document.createElement('div');
                growth.className = 'vina3-kpi';
                growth.innerHTML = '<p class="vina3-kpi-label">Revenue Growth</p><p class="vina3-kpi-value">+8.1%</p>';
                kpis.appendChild(growth);

                var kpiNodes = Array.prototype.slice.call(kpis.querySelectorAll('.vina3-kpi'));
                var trendPath = trend.querySelector('path');

                function renderReason(host, nodes, t, start, step, end) {
                    var active = t >= start && t < end;
                    host.hidden = !active;
                    if (!active) return;
                    host.style.paddingTop = '13px';
                    host.style.paddingBottom = '13px';
                    nodes.forEach(function (node, i) {
                        fade(node, t, start + i * step, 360, 6);
                        node.classList.toggle('done', t >= start + (i + 1) * step);
                    });
                }

                if (reduced) {
                    shell.style.opacity = 1;
                    shell.style.transform = 'none';
                    welcome.hidden = true;
                    conversation.hidden = false;
                    reason1.hidden = true;
                    reason2.hidden = true;
                    answer.hidden = false;
                    q2.hidden = false;
                    summary.hidden = false;
                    ansA.textContent = responseA;
                    ansB.textContent = responseB;
                    products.forEach(function (el) {
                        el.style.opacity = 1;
                        el.style.transform = 'none';
                    });
                    kpiNodes.forEach(function (el) {
                        el.style.opacity = 1;
                        el.style.transform = 'none';
                    });
                    trendPath.setAttribute('stroke-dashoffset', '0');
                    return;
                }

                var start = null;

                function tick(now) {
                    if (start === null) start = now;
                    var t = (now - start) % LOOP;

                    fade(shell, t, 0, 800, 12);

                    var inConversation = t >= T.sent;
                    welcome.hidden = inConversation;
                    conversation.hidden = !inConversation;

                    if (!inConversation) {
                        cards.forEach(function (card, i) {
                            fade(card, t, T.cards + i * 120, 520, 8);
                            card.classList.toggle('is-hover', i === 0 && t >= T.hover && t < T.sent - 100);
                        });
                    } else {
                        fade(q1, t, T.sent, 420, 8);
                        renderReason(reason1, r1, t, T.reason1, 460, T.respond + 150);

                        answer.hidden = t < T.respond;
                        if (!answer.hidden) {
                            ansA.textContent = responseA.slice(0, Math.round(ease(progress(t, T.respond, T.respond + 650)) * responseA.length));
                            ansB.textContent = responseB.slice(0, Math.round(ease(progress(t, T.respond + 420, T.respond + 1120)) * responseB.length));
                            kpiNodes.forEach(function (node, i) {
                                fade(node, t, T.kpi + i * 110, 450, 7);
                            });
                            trendPath.setAttribute('stroke-dashoffset', 160 - 160 * ease(progress(t, T.kpi + 350, T.kpi + 1500)));
                        }

                        q2.hidden = t < T.send2;
                        if (!q2.hidden) fade(q2, t, T.send2, 420, 8);
                        renderReason(reason2, r2, t, T.reason2, 390, T.products + 100);

                        summary.hidden = t < T.products;
                        if (!summary.hidden) fade(summary, t, T.products, 480, 8);
                    }

                    var typed = typedText.slice(0, Math.round(ease(progress(t, T.typeStart, T.typeEnd)) * typedText.length));
                    var typing = t >= T.typeStart && t < T.send2;
                    inputText.innerHTML = typing ? typed + '<span class="vina3-caret"></span>' : '<span class="vina3-ph">Ask vina about your business…</span>';
                    inputBar.classList.toggle('is-typing', typing);
                    send.classList.toggle('is-sending', t >= T.send2 - 180 && t < T.send2 + 380);

                    products.forEach(function (el, i) {
                        var p = ease(progress(t, T.products + i * 130, T.products + i * 130 + 720));
                        el.style.opacity = p;
                        el.style.transform = 'translateY(' + ((1 - p) * -22) + 'px) scale(' + (.97 + p * .03) + ')';
                    });

                    slideEl.vina3RafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
                }

                slideEl.vina3RafId = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
            }

            function initializeActiveHeroSlide(swiperInstance) {
                var activeSlide = swiperInstance.slides[swiperInstance.activeIndex];
                if (!activeSlide) return;

                Array.prototype.forEach.call(swiperInstance.slides, function (slide) {
                    if (slide !== activeSlide) {
                        stopFYSlideAnimation(slide);
                        stopSystemSlideAnimation(slide);
                        stopVinaSlideAnimation(slide);
                    }
                });

                if (activeSlide.classList.contains('hero-fy-slide')) {
                    initFYSlideAnimation(activeSlide);
                    return;
                }

                if (activeSlide.classList.contains('hero-brand-slide')) {
                    initSlideAnimation(activeSlide);
                    return;
                }

                if (activeSlide.classList.contains('hero-system-slide')) {
                    initSystemSlideAnimation(activeSlide);
                    return;
                }

                if (activeSlide.classList.contains('hero-vina-slide')) {
                    initVinaSlideAnimation(activeSlide);
                }
            }


            function initHeroSwiperDynamicHeight(swiper) {
                if (!swiper) return;

                var updateHeight = function () {
                    var activeSlide = swiper.slides[swiper.activeIndex];

                    if (!activeSlide) return;

                    /*
                     * Temporarily remove the manually applied wrapper height
                     * so we can measure the active slide's natural height.
                     */
                    var previousHeight = swiper.wrapperEl.style.height;
                    swiper.wrapperEl.style.height = 'auto';

                    requestAnimationFrame(function () {

                        var height = activeSlide.getBoundingClientRect().height;

                        /*
                         * Measure the actual Bootstrap row.
                         * This prevents the Swiper wrapper's previous height
                         * from affecting the calculation.
                         */
                        var row = activeSlide.querySelector('.row');

                        if (row) {
                            var rowHeight = row.getBoundingClientRect().height;

                            if (rowHeight > 0) {
                                height = rowHeight;
                            }
                        }

                        if (!height || height < 1) {
                            swiper.wrapperEl.style.height = previousHeight;
                            return;
                        }

                        /*
                         * Set the wrapper to exactly the height
                         * required by the current slide.
                         */
                        swiper.wrapperEl.style.height = (Math.ceil(height) + 50) + 'px';

                        /*
                         * Keep Swiper's internal dimensions synchronized.
                         */
                        swiper.updateSlides();
                    });
                };

                swiper.updateHeroHeight = updateHeight;

                /*
                 * Observe every slide.
                 * This is important because your slides contain
                 * dynamically changing/animated content.
                 */
                var observer = new ResizeObserver(function (entries) {

                    var activeSlide = swiper.slides[swiper.activeIndex];

                    if (!activeSlide) return;

                    var changedActiveSlide = entries.some(function (entry) {
                        return entry.target === activeSlide ||
                            activeSlide.contains(entry.target);
                    });

                    if (changedActiveSlide) {
                        updateHeight();
                    }
                });

                Array.prototype.forEach.call(swiper.slides, function (slide) {

                    /*
                     * Observe the complete slide.
                     */
                    observer.observe(slide);

                    /*
                     * Observe the Bootstrap row.
                     */
                    var row = slide.querySelector('.row');

                    if (row) {
                        observer.observe(row);
                    }

                    /*
                     * Observe VINA's animated scene.
                     */
                    var vinaScene = slide.querySelector('.vina3-scene');

                    if (vinaScene) {
                        observer.observe(vinaScene);
                    }
                });

                swiper.heroHeightObserver = observer;

                /*
                 * Initial calculation.
                 */
                updateHeight();

                /*
                 * Recalculate whenever the viewport changes.
                 */
                if (!swiper.heroHeightResizeHandler) {

                    swiper.heroHeightResizeHandler = function () {
                        updateHeight();
                    };

                    window.addEventListener(
                        'resize',
                        swiper.heroHeightResizeHandler
                    );
                }
            }

            new Swiper('.heroSwiper', {
                loop: true,
                speed: 600,

                /*
                 * We are handling the height ourselves.
                 */
                autoHeight: false,

                pagination: {
                    el: '.swiper-pagination',
                    clickable: true,
                },

                navigation: {
                    nextEl: '.swiper-button-next',
                    prevEl: '.swiper-button-prev',
                },

                on: {
                    init: function () {
                        initializeActiveHeroSlide(this);

                        initHeroSwiperDynamicHeight(this);
                    },

                    slideChangeTransitionStart: function () {
                        initializeActiveHeroSlide(this);

                        if (this.updateHeroHeight) {
                            this.updateHeroHeight();
                        }
                    },

                    slideChangeTransitionEnd: function () {
                        if (this.updateHeroHeight) {
                            this.updateHeroHeight();
                        }
                    }
                }
            });

        })();
    </script>
    <!-- <script>
        // JS to automatically duplicate content for a seamless loop
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
            const track = document.getElementById("marqueeTrack");
            // Clone all original brand elements and append them to the track
            const clone = track.innerHTML;
            track.innerHTML += clone;
        });
    </script> -->

    <script>
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

            // Explicit logo arrays per slot (Add as many logos as you want to each slot)
            const slotPools = [
                // Slot 0 (e.g. 5 Logos)
                [
                    { name: 'Google Logo', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/reliance.png' },
                    { name: 'Apple Logo', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/gap.png' },
                ],

                // Slot 1 (e.g. 6 Logos)
                [
                    { name: 'Lee Cooper', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/lee-cooper.png' },
                    { name: 'John Players', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/john-players.png' },
                ],

                // Slot 2 (e.g. 4 Logos)
                [
                    { name: 'Prashant Corner', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/prashant-corner.png' },
                    { name: 'Ashwins Sweets', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/ashwins-sweets.png' },
                ],

                // Slot 3 (e.g. 5 Logos)
                [
                    { name: 'Man Company', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/man-company.png' },
                    { name: 'Mars Cosmetics', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/mars-cosmetics.png' },
                ],

                // Slot 4 (e.g. 4 Logos)
                [
                    { name: 'Just dogs', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/just-dogs.png' },
                    { name: 'Ladrech Chocolatier Suisse', url: 'assets-new/images/logos-new/ladrech-cocolatier-suisse.png' },
                ]
            ];

            // Automatically track active logo index for every slot
            const poolIndices = new Array(slotPools.length).fill(0);

            // Render initial starting logo for each slot
            slotPools.forEach((pool, i) => {
                const slotContainer = document.getElementById(`slot-${i}`);
                if (slotContainer && pool.length > 0) {
                    const initialLogo = pool[0];
                    const logoItem = document.createElement('div');
                    logoItem.className = 'logo-item active';
                    logoItem.innerHTML = `<img src="${initialLogo.url}" alt="${initialLogo.name}">`;
                    slotContainer.appendChild(logoItem);
                }
            });

            // Vertical Scroll Transition Handler
            function triggerScrollUpChange(slotIndex) {
                const slotContainer = document.getElementById(`slot-${slotIndex}`);
                const pool = slotPools[slotIndex];

                // Skip animation if slot is missing or has 1 or 0 logos
                if (!slotContainer || !pool || pool.length <= 1) return;

                const currentActiveItem = slotContainer.querySelector('.logo-item.active');

                // Advance index for this slot
                poolIndices[slotIndex] = (poolIndices[slotIndex] + 1) % pool.length;
                const nextLogo = pool[poolIndices[slotIndex]];

                // Create new element at the bottom out of viewport
                const newLogoItem = document.createElement('div');
                newLogoItem.className = 'logo-item enter-bottom';
                newLogoItem.innerHTML = `<img src="${nextLogo.url}" alt="${nextLogo.name}">`;
                slotContainer.appendChild(newLogoItem);

                // Force DOM Reflow
                void newLogoItem.offsetWidth;

                // Perform transition
                if (currentActiveItem) {
                    currentActiveItem.classList.remove('active');
                    currentActiveItem.classList.add('exit-up');
                }

                newLogoItem.classList.remove('enter-bottom');
                newLogoItem.classList.add('active');

                // Cleanup old node post animation
                setTimeout(() => {
                    if (currentActiveItem && currentActiveItem.parentNode) {
                        currentActiveItem.parentNode.removeChild(currentActiveItem);
                    }
                }, 550);
            }

            // Slot transition sequence across all 5 slots (Slot 0 -> 2 -> 1 -> 4 -> 3)
            const slotSequence = [0, 2, 1, 4, 3];
            let seqIndex = 0;

            setInterval(() => {
                const slotToAnimate = slotSequence[seqIndex];
                triggerScrollUpChange(slotToAnimate);
                seqIndex = (seqIndex + 1) % slotSequence.length;
            }, 1800);
        });
    </script>
</body>
